# contribution tracker

[contributions.tscircuit.com](https://contributions.tscircuit.com) ・ [tscircuit.com](https://tscircuit.com) ・ [Contribution Overviews](./contribution-overviews/) ・ [Changelogs](./changelogs/)

Generates weekly contribution overviews for tscircuit contributors. Check out all
the [contribution overviews here](./contribution-overviews/)
You can find AI-generated monthly changelogs in the [changelogs directory](./changelogs/)

- All PRs in the tscircuit org are scanned/summarized via an LLM
- The LLM classifies each Diff/PR as into a set of attributes for scoring
- All the PRs, summaries, and classifications are organized into charts and tables for [the website](https://contributions.tscircuit.com)

> Want to run locally? See the [Development Section](#development)

## Changes by Repository

### [tscircuit/circuit-json-util](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-util)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |
|------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| [#200](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-util/pull/200) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | imrishabh18 | Extends the existing analyzers two-pad convention to infer pin 1 orientation for longer straight pad rows with unique, consecutive pin numbers, resolving supplier placement preparation failures. |
| [#204](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-util/pull/204) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Transforms explicit solder-mask opening geometry to ensure that their pads move correctly with cached or packed PCB components, preserving local dimensions, IDs, ownership, and attachment face while handling various shapes and orientations. |
| [#206](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-util/pull/206) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | AnasSarkiz | Fixes the issue where polygon solder paste remains at its original position during PCB placement, ensuring that paste stays aligned with its pad by applying the existing point transform to copper outlines, paste outlines, and inner openings. |
| [#212](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-util/pull/212) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | mohan-bee | Aligns polygon, path, and rectangular cutouts with transformed PCB geometry by applying transformations to their coordinates and orientations. |
| [#209](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-util/pull/209) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | mohan-bee | Fixes the issue where rectangular and circular cutouts do not move with the PCB geometry, ensuring proper alignment during transformations. |
| [#208](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-util/pull/208) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | mohan-bee | Fixes the issue where rectangular cutouts do not move with the footprint geometry during repositioning, ensuring accurate PCB representation. |

### [tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |
|------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| [#2820](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2820) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | imrishabh18 | Reduces overhead in port ownership lookup and cache key construction by indexing ownership once per solver and buffering typed serialization before hashing. |
| [#2776](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2776) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | imrishabh18 | Reduces DRC errors for bugreport107 to 58 on macOS and 59 on Linux by improving clearance and routing logic in the autorouter. |
| [#2780](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2780) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | imrishabh18 | Reduces DRC errors in bugreport107 from 82 to 63 on macOS and 65 on Linux by implementing a final whole-board clearance projection that moves wire bends and vias together. |
| [#2769](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2769) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | imrishabh18 | Long, straight trace spans can remain too close to copper because the clearance projector only moves existing vertices and their endpoints are locked. Add bounded subdivisions to constant-width spans on routes implicated in DRC errors, then run a refinement pass after the existing independent wire repairs. This gives the solver nearby vertices to bend while retaining terminals, junctions, widths, and via sites. |
| [#2778](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2778) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | imrishabh18 | Reduces DRC errors in bugreport107 by adjusting clearance segment lengths to improve pad-to-trace clearance without altering existing copper or connections. |
| [#2835](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2835) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Optimizes the HD endpoint geometry and seed scoring by precomputing segment geometry per force pass, reusing repeated endpoint projections, and rejecting duplicate native layouts before scoring. |
| [#2836](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2836) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Reduces overhead in the repair process by optimizing the query and evaluator setup, focusing on reusing net decisions and improving context handling. |
| [#2834](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2834) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Reduces routing overhead by optimizing port routing processes, including region scanning, workspace allocation, and statistics management, while maintaining existing routing policies and behaviors. |
| [#2823](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2823) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | The default local Pipeline 9 returned a solved Muse e-paper result with 0.135411 mm between different-net via copper lands, below the boards 0.15 mm pad-edge rule. This change routes the identical captured input with zero DRC errors, preserving all 92 vias 0.3 mm drills and 0.5 mm copper diameters. Fixes 2822. The reproduction is retained in this PR; its original characterization-only commit is 341c786e51765f3f4ff9ef8f8278244eedb1758d. The failure belongs to autorouting and repair, and the board contains no copper pours. The pinned checks 0.0.222 checker only checked drill gaps, while the projection solver used the relaxed 0.1 mm via clearance. The consuming build also checks copper. Updating the checker alone exposes the failure but leaves this pair unrepaired. Upgrade checks to 0.0.233 so reference DRC measures different-net via copper on shared layers. Preserve the declared minPadEdgeToPadEdgeClearance on the reference DRC board and forward it into clearance projection. Keep drill clearance separate and retain the existing precision margin for coupled projection. Keep the indexed engines search heuristic separate: its single via copper gap applies to both same-net and different-net pairs, while the electrical pad rule applies to different nets. Reference DRC checks the declared rules before accepting candidates. Turn the captured full-board reproduction into an unconditional zero-DRC regression with independent copper-gap measurements. Add focused cases with distinct copper, trace and drill rules, including terminal and input preservation. Correct the older unit case that treated overlapping copper as legal merely because drill clearance passed. No new repair stage, report-specific coordinates, post-routing correction, or DRC tolerance relaxation is added. Validation: 43 focused tests pass, covering solver repair, reference DRC, clearance projection, continuity, differential pairs and the beforeafter Muse snapshots. The same-net search regression fails under the previous rule and passes with the correction. TypeScript check and library build pass. The complete Muse tsci build --site passes all 23 board checks, with zero circuit errorswarnings. A local CLI adapter returns the built fixed solvers output unchanged. Its main-phase SRJ is deep-equal to the original failing capture; closest different-net via copper gap is 0.15 mm. The original published Muse project is preserved. The fix has not been released to npm or merged. The fixtures REPORT.md contains the input provenance, beforeafter measurements, and reproduction command.  Visual comparison Same captured SRJ, cache disabled, effort 1, 92 physical vias with 0.3 mm drills and 0.5 mm copper. Both views use the same current checker and the declared 0.15 mm copper rule. Each image includes the full-board overview and the affected BOOST_SW  RESE vias at the same magnification. The translucent rings add half the required copper clearance to each via. Original reproduction: 1 DRC error, 0.135411 mm copper gap. Frozen, unmodified output freshly captured from published capacity-autorouter 0.0.953. !Original reproduction snapshot(https:raw.githubusercontent.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouter06059f45d085411c7909da90c964022f169b32c8testsrepro__snapshots__pipeline9-muse-eink-original-output.snap.svg) Fix: 0 DRC errors, 0.150000 mm copper gap. | [#2829](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2829) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Reduces high-density routing overhead by sharing geometry and caching proven-free planar points, improving efficiency in routing calculations. |
| [#2811](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2811) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Board 1726 currently leaves about 55 relaxed DRC errors after Pipeline 9 routing. This change clears the goal repro to zero relaxed and declared-clearance errors, including after final power-trace expansion. Regional repair uses one effort-scaled policy for every board. It starts with a small allowance and earns additional bounded search only when a physically valid candidate reduces the whole-board reference DRC count. The previous route-count threshold is removed. Required-clearance projection runs before precision-margin refinement so exact-fit channels can be repaired without first asking for infeasible extra slack. All added repair work uses persistent Pipeline subsolvers. Exact, B01, regional search, clearance projection, reported-via merging, and repair04 path searches expose their active child and advance it once per parent step. No added nested solve(), synchronous child-step drains, or generators. The incremental repair04 classes are proposed upstream in repair04 fix PR 26(https:github.comtscircuitrepair04pull26), which targets main and includes the visual fixtures captured in repro PR 27(https:github.comtscircuitrepair04pull27). The repro PR captures 13 real PCB close-ups; the fix updates eight SVG snapshots with the same input panels, viewports, colors, and computed measurements. Both upstream PRs have green CI. This PR pins the immutable 422b7546 source commit and has no Bun patch or patchedDependencies. Existing synchronous APIs remain intact; the new classes use bounded preparation, search, and sweep batches. When a via pair needs more separation while touching fixed pads, projection removes the inward movement and retains feasible motion along the pad boundary. This applies only to via-to-via constraints; pad and wire constraints retain their existing movement behavior. Conservative rounding correction and uniform ray shortening handle rotated pads and board limits while preserving the exact physical guards, locked endpoints, displacement limits, and solver budgets. The upstream PR adds repro tests and snapshots while keeping all 27 library files byte-identical to the pinned source. Source review confirms the transfer preserves the tested patchs behavior. Full goal CI on the patch-free head(https:github.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouteractionsruns36975615842job110738689115) passed all eight assertions in 827.5s, including both zero-DRC checks and the final snapshot. Cap routing, force-improvement, and repair tuning at 1x while preserving lower-effort behavior. Fix the existing benchmark CLI truncating --effort 1.5 to 1, and verify the effective effort in every comparison report. Higher-effort snapshot updates retain their DRC assertions and use separate LinuxmacOS expectations where routes differ. Full dataset18 comparison completed successfully. All 16 samples solved and passed relaxed DRC at every effort, with zero timeouts and no per-sample via-count increases. The PR-comment command becomes available when the dispatcherparser changes reach the default branch. Pre-merge comparison can be dispatched through the existing Autorouting Benchmark workflow on this branch with effort_comparetrue, the full commit SHA, and this PR number. | [#2740](https://github.com/tscircuit/tscircuit-autorouter/pull/2740) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | Abse2001 | Summary Fix through-via handling in Pipeline 9 and its Repair03 dependency. This PR targets main directly and contains the fix, focused regression tests, related existing-testsnapshot updates, and CI timeout classification for the existing SRJ18 sample 2 tests. It does not add the full Game Boy repro, its input, or its snapshot. The Game Boy stays in validation-only repro 2741(https:github.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouterpull2741), not for merging. No merge or auto-merge has been requested.  Bug When allowBlindAndBuriedVias is false or omitted, a via connecting top to inner1 still occupies every copper layer. Its signal transition does not define its drill span. Using only the signal layers lets a foreign net pass through the via on inner2 or bottom.  Fix Pipeline 9 fixed-copper geometry, nodeB01 obstacle selection, and regional collision checks use the existing board via policy. Through vias reserve all board layers; explicitly allowed blindburied vias retain their limited span. The geometry cache includes layer count and via policy. Repair03 is pinned to ac744fcc(https:github.comAbse2001high-density-repair03commitac744fcc8cc4e83fbf12769507e963c974f5932f): an integration commit on top of the existing cbbc86a3 pin, porting merged 143(https:github.comtscircuithigh-density-repair03pull143) drill-span handling and still-open 144(https:github.comtscircuithigh-density-repair03pull144), which refreshes via point indexes after a trace detour. Existing optimizations and invalid-endpoint checks are preserved. This is a fork integration pin, not an upstream release or the exact head of 144. Current main already includes the clearance-margin transition-identity correction and the newer trace-simplification via-preservation fix from 2779(https:github.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouterpull2779). The merge retains both; the transition-identity production code is no longer an additional diff in this PR. Signal endpoints and component-owned through-obstacle geometry are unchanged. No board-specific routing cases, new feature flags, or suppressed invariant failures.  Dependency and merge order Review and merge Repair03 144 first, then replace the integration pin with an upstream commit containing both fixes and the existing pinned behavior before merging this autorouter PR. Revalidate after changing the pin; do not assume another branch or package version is equivalent. 143 is merged; 144 was still open when checked on September 30. No PR will be merged automatically.  Focused regression tests pipeline9-fixed-via-drill-span: falsedefaulttrue via policy, every copper layer, collisions in both argument orders, layer-countcache changes, and invalid transitions. pipeline9-b01-through-via-obstacle: an actual bottom-layer route avoids a top-to-inner1 through via; explicitly permitting blind vias makes bottom available. Completion, clearance, and endpoints are checked. Its one native snapshot shows captured B01 solver states before and after routing, following Seves snapshot guidance(https:github.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouterpull2193pullrequestreview-4998204680). pipeline9-clearance-margin-through-via-transition: an inner-layer signal transition remains valid inside a through via without losing transition identity. After merging main and installing the actual merged dependencies, these three tests plus mains pipeline9-clearance-margin-tracks-via-identity passed locally: 4 tests, 73 assertions. No full-board routing or dataset benchmarks were run locally.  Timeout investigation and optimization  September 30 Latest head 13dc4a0f adds only an early geometry rejection in getConnectedPadSides plus one focused regression test. It skips net-connectivity lookups for pads that cannot contain the route terminal. The same layer test, 0.001 mm tolerance, connected-net predicate, output ordering, routing rules and dependency pins remain unchanged. The new test failed before the optimization (4 unnecessary lookups) and passes afterward. Three focused tests pass with 36 assertions. The previous standard SRJ18 benchmark(https:github.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouterpull2740issuecomment-5915973380) at 5680cbdc did regress completion from 1616 to 1416: samples 14 and 15 timed out at 360 seconds in joint repair. Dataset01 stayed 8585 complete and DRC-passing. The new benchmark below follows an actual optimization, not an unchanged retry; the earlier failures and sample14s limited timeout margin remain relevant. GitHub CPU profiling(https:github.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouteractionsruns36756273653) compared exact main e85fb193 and PR 5680cbdc with identical sample inputs. All four serial diagnostic solves completed with zero relaxed DRCs, but this is a different runnerworkload from the normal eight-worker benchmark, not proof that its timeout regression is resolved. Sample14 joint-repair wall time rose from 75.52 to 107.39 seconds. CPU samples locate most additional work in bounded regional clearance search (54.97 to 71.39 sampled seconds) and regional B01 repair (7.83 to 22.02). Repair03 portfolio time was only about 7.35 to 7.70 seconds; its via-index refresh was not the dominant measured cost. Sample15 serial total time was approximately unchanged (197.49 to 196.87 seconds). Its profile nevertheless identified about 6.01 sampled seconds of net lookup work beneath the pad-side helper, motivating the geometry-first change. This does not remove sample14s larger clearance-search cost. The completed beforeafter optimization profile(https:github.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouteractionsruns36760525317) compares old PR 5680cbdc (directory label main, not repository main) against 13dc4a0f. Both samples have byte-identical input, route JSON and DRC JSON before and after, unchanged routing iterations and clearance-search work counts, and zero relaxed DRCs. Sample14 remains 238 traces281 vias; sample15 remains 461 traces349 vias. Diagnostic instrumentation stays outside this PR. Sample14 pad-helper inclusive sampled CPU time fell 1.892 to 0.061 seconds, but serial total time was 249.972 to 254.176 seconds (1.7). Sample15 helper CPU fell 8.917 to 0.060 seconds, with serial total time 201.698 to 190.490 seconds (-5.6). This confirms the unnecessary lookup cost was removed without changing copper; it does not show a uniform end-to-end speedup. These are profiler measurements on a serial runner, not the standard benchmark score. | [#4368](https://github.com/tscircuit/core/pull/4368) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | A chip can declare requiresVoltage: 1.8V while its fetched part metadata says requires_voltage: 3.3. Rendering now compares explicit pinAttributes with the original fetched facts, before overrides are merged, and emits one warning per chip with declaredfetched values for up to three affected pins plus a remaining count. The check runs in each chips source lifecycle, using the same physical-pin and alias matching as source rendering. It reuses imported metadata or fetches datasheet information through the configured parts engine for a custom footprint or manufacturer part number. Fetching comparison facts does not import the official footprint or replace the users overrides. The board no longer orchestrates chip pin-specification checks. Share pending, successful, missing, and failed metadata lookups for repeated chips within a circuit instance, keyed by suppliermanufacturer part numbers and fetch implementation. Supplier imports and custom-footprint validation share the same fallback sequence; a new circuit instance can retry. Fallback diagnostics still use each chips own name. Run this validation and its additional metadata fetches only for the chip primitive. Resistor, capacitor, inductor, crystal, and connector primitives retain their existing behavior. Physical passives declared as chip are still included. Normalize voltage units and compare explicit booleans, including false and zero. Omitted declarations and unknown fetched fields are not mismatches. Compare declared capabilities with fetched support. Runtime selections may differ from fetched defaults, but selecting an explicitly unsupported mode or capability warns. Display properties and intentional noConnect choices are excluded. Updateremove the same aggregate diagnostic on rechecks. Reuse source_component_pins_underspecified_warning for Circuit JSON compatibility; a conflict summary replaces legacy chip-pin warnings. Otherwise the existing all-underspecified, missing-power, and missing-ground checks remain in the chip lifecycle. Respect DRC controls and avoid repeating a supplier footprints datasheet request after a timeout or legacy-engine fallback. Without fetched electrical facts, the comparison cannot verify datasheet accuracy. Eighteen regression tests cover imports, custom footprints, rendering without a board or official footprint, aliases, unit equivalence, capabilities, unavailableambiguous facts, DRC controls, immutability, warning updates, shared requests and failures, cache isolation, retries in a fresh circuit, and exclusion of passives. Exactly two new snapshots show fetched facts beside the users code and explain the generated result inside the image. !Custom footprint with conflicting pin declarations(https:raw.githubusercontent.comtscircuitcorefeatchip-pin-attributes-validationtestscomponentsnormal-components__snapshots__chip-pin-attributes-fetched-mismatch-schematic.snap.svg) !Matching declarations and omitted attributes(https:raw.githubusercontent.comtscircuitcorefeatchip-pin-attributes-validationtestscomponentsnormal-components__snapshots__chip-pin-attributes-fetched-match-schematic.snap.svg) Public-board compatibility study (details(https:github.comtscircuitcoreblobfeatchip-pin-attributes-validationdocschip-pin-attributes-validation.mdpublic-board-compatibility-study)): Inspected six public boards and replayed their instantiated component declarations through source-only rendering. The RP2040 controllers 13 attributed chip instances share 10 distinct lookups; Gameboy and AM3352RAM add none. F1C100S declarations produce one warning for swapped PE0PE1 IC and TPY1TPY2 UARTSPI capabilities. Confirmed all four against Allwinners PDF and added a regression preserving physical pin numbers and shared requests. Observed limitations: antennacrystal devices represented as chip still get lookups; the JLC metadata flow also requests supplier geometry and model assets; missing metadata, supplier misses, and underscore-form manufacturer identities leave chips unverified. Full-board layoutrender compatibility was not established; the successful checks are isolated declaration replays. Validation: Focused comparison, fetch caching, passive exclusion, and datasheetlegacy fallback regressions: 23 passing. Prior broader component, subcircuit, imported-metadata, and DRC regression run: 507 passing, 4 skipped; this update adds a passing public-board regression and documentation only. bunx tsc --noEmit and bun run build. |

| [#4324](https://github.com/tscircuit/core/pull/4324) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Core now implements the motor and board-mount props added in tscircuitprops893. Authors can assemble a controller and a NEMA motor without creating a model URL or manually calculating CAD offsets: tsx assembly.device assembly.motor nameNEMA17 standardnema17  Rp2040MotorController mountedToNEMA17.backface mountGap6mm  assembly.device  The controller must forward these props to its board. standard supports NEMA81723; model accepts a custom NEMA modelprinter string instead. The optional shaftFacingDirection defaults to z and supports all six signed axes. Core parses modelprinter specs and resolves modelcdn GLBs; mesh generation remains in jscad-electronics. Backface mounting uses the actual model body length and finalized PCB thickness to measure the requested gap from the nearest board surface. Boards keep their existing PCB coordinates; the motor follows the board center and in-plane rotation. Mount references resolve in the nearest assembly device, including forward references, with errors for missingambiguous targets and conflicting placements. Board mounting currently supports z and z-. Sideways motors render, but sideways board mounts report a clear error until Circuit JSON and the renderers support rotated PCB boards. Validation: 32 assemblynamespacecontainer tests pass, including ten new motor tests with annotated PNG snapshots pairing TSX on the left with the render on the right. Shaft orientations, NEMA standards and backface mounting directions each have one combined comparison image per test file; the PCB-disabled case explicitly shows only R1 in the schematic. Geometry assertions measure exported GLB bounds for all standards, all six directions, custom dimensions, and the 6 mm surface gap. All ten CI test shards, typecheck, package buildpreview, dist smoke tests and dependency checks pass. The RP2040 controller placement build also succeeds with the new API: a 1.6 mm PCB and 6 mm rear clearance produce the NEMA17 origin at Z  -44.8 mm. All 1,901 emitted PCB records match an unmounted build under the same core. This integration check disables routing: the projects saved route plan from an older core rejects the current routing input and needs a separate migration. The unmodified controller build without an assembly also rejects the route plan under current core. |

| [#4326](https://github.com/tscircuit/core/pull/4326) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Placed parts emit source_missing_manufacturer_part_number_warning only when they have neither a manufacturer part number nor a non-empty supplier part number after parts-engine resolution finishes. For example, a resistor whose engine lookup returns  jlcpcb: C123  does not need an explicit MPN; an empty or failed lookup leaves the part unresolved and produces a warning. Explicit supplier part numbers also satisfy the check. Resistors, capacitors, and inductors follow the same resolution rule as other components, and doNotPlace remains exempt. The new warning phase runs after PartsEngineRender and waits for its asynchronous effects plus footprint fetches. The parts-engine findPart API returns supplier IDs, so the check uses those matches without inventing or copying an MPN. It respects DRC disabling, emits one warning per source component with connector standard metadata, and removes stale warnings when a later update resolves a part. All part renderers resolve mpn, mfn, and manufacturerPartNumber consistently into manufacturer_part_number. This also fixes previously ignored mfn aliases on chips, pinouts, jumpers, and solder jumpers, and uses the resolved number for schematic labels and connector parts-engine lookups. Props prerequisite https:github.comtscircuitpropspull895 is merged and released in tscircuitprops0.0.680. Core follows current mains 0.0.681 dev dependency and override, which include the alias API. Peer dependencies retain  as required by the repositorys internal-package policy. Validation uses the published props package. The warning-schema prerequisite https:github.comtscircuitcircuit-jsonpull856 is also merged and released in circuit-json0.0.511. Core uses that minimum in its dev dependency and override. Validation uses the published packages, with no local prerequisite builds. Validation: all CI checks pass on the latest commit, including all ten test shards, type checking, dependency policy, formatting, package preview, and distribution smoke tests. All 18 focused MPNparts-engine tests also pass locally. The local componentbase-componentroot-circuit run with Bun 1.3.2 had 451 passing tests, 3 skips, and one pre-existing numeric snapshot formatting difference in pin-header-pcbOrientation.test.tsx; CIs Bun version passes it. Coverage checks aliases across 23 part types, pending lookups, successful automatic passive selection, emptyfailed lookups, cache hits, disabled engines, standard connector selection, explicitemptyblank supplier IDs, stale-warning removal, ownershipdeduplication, DRC disabling, and doNotPlace exemptions. EasyEDA imports already emit manufacturerPartNumber, which satisfies this check. Additional fallbackskip-path regression coverage: https:github.comtscircuitclipull5122. No generator changes are required. |

| [#4232](https://github.com/tscircuit/core/pull/4232) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | autoroutingphase autorouterbus_lanes  now routes the AM3352RAM fixture from TSX, with local pad-to-via dogbones when a layer transition is needed. Existing fanout exits are used directly. The fixture contains no custom algorithm and no saved route geometry. The solver preserves single-layer carriers, bus length matching, coupled differential pairs, curved tuning, and reduced unnecessary turns. The regression requires all 47 traces and zero errors, and checks pair gapskew, bus skew, layer transitions, detour and turn limits. Only fully routed snapshots are included. Async rendering now waits for completed effects instead of repeatedly traversing idle component trees; bus routing uses immediate task scheduling on NodeBun. A focused footprint lifecycle regression covers the idle behavior. The earlier focused suite passed 22 tests  278 assertions. Revalidation with the published solver 0.0.5 passes all 169 AM3352 assertions; the three completed signal-layer snapshots were regenerated and visually inspected. The production dependency now uses published bus-lanes-solver 0.0.5 from jscdn and connectivity-map 0.0.33. The preview workflow no longer substitutes a different solver. A separate dependency-only PR 4267(https:github.comtscircuitcorepull4267) allows these merged performance improvements to propagate through core  eval  downstream releases independently of this feature. No new prop or props release is required. autoroutingphase autorouterbus_lanes  automatically adds local dogbones only at unrouted component-pad endpoints when needed to reach the selected layer. Supplied fanout exits keep their existing layers and are never dogboned again. The rejected busLanesFanout API and props preview dependency have been removed; props PR 875 is closed. Validation after removing the option: all seven bus_lanes integration cases pass across the focused runs, including the full AM3352 test (169 assertions), automatic pad dogbones, preservation of saved fanouts without added vias, and rejection of incompatible existing fanout layers. ESM and declaration builds pass with published props. Removed the old expected-failure snapshot; no unrouted artifacts are added. AM3352 with the published solver passes locally in 23.1 seconds (47 traces, zero DRC errors, all quality gates). The hosted SVG build remains above 30 seconds; local timing is not evidence of deployed performance. |

### [tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |
|------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| [#183](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/183) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | MustafaMulla29 | Schematic shrink suggestions can ignore longer pin banks, introduce corner-label collisions, and mistake inline symbol artwork for empty space. Both padding and width suggestions now validate all pin banks, label geometry and the footprint of inline artwork before recommending a smaller box. The shared validator derives minimum dimensions from Circuit JSON pin sides, positions, spacing and displayed label geometry. It preserves a 0.2 schematic-unit gap between labels on different sides and retains the existing 0.02 collision clearance. Combined and single-axis proposals are checked together, preserving valid follow-up suggestions instead of discarding an entire resize when only one axis is unsafe. Inline paths, circles and rectangles emitted by core are measured from their coordinates and stroke widths, linked by schematic_component_id. Their bounding footprint participates in the same containment and clearance constraints as labels. There is no connector-standard exception, artwork-based component exclusion, name matching or guessed scaling formula. The calculation conservatively preserves the currently rendered drawing footprint; when core rescales artwork during a re-render, reanalysis can find a further valid reduction. For the real J_PD fixture, reducing width from 8 to 1.4 leaves a height warning with a safe 3.10 suggestion. The input-sheet fixture retains all four valid padding warnings. The existing USB-C symbols on the BLDC and Trellis boards receive no shrink suggestion. The native USB-C TSX test also exercises an oversized version: its suggestions preserve artwork and labels, and repeated native re-renders reach a compact state with zero findings. Validation: All 145 tests pass; TypeScript and formatting pass, with two existing large-fixture formatting warnings. Native TSX re-renders check pin clearance, label gaps, artwork containment and labelartwork separation. All eight mirrored corner cases assert width and height at translated positions, with one representative stacked snapshot per axis. Stacked snapshots retain every intermediate warning and final issue count. Stacked on 182. Merge the reproduction first, then retarget this PR to main. The PCB project itself is unchanged. |
| [#159](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/159) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | MustafaMulla29 | Adds a new solver to detect capacitors that are placed far from their connected chip pins, ensuring they are readable beside those pins for better schematic clarity. |
| [#156](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/156) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | MustafaMulla29 | Adds PiFilterPlacementSolver  PiFilterComponentsNotGrouped for a series inductor separated from its two grounded shunt capacitors, reporting findings on each unchanged real repro and highlighting all three parts with matching numbered diagnostics. |
| [#127](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/127) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | MustafaMulla29 | Adds RelayFlybackDiodePlacementSolver, reporting FlybackDiodeSeparatedFromRelayCoil when a local, label-connected protection diode is placed away from its relay coil. |
| [#124](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/124) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | MustafaMulla29 | Adds CurrentSenseShuntPlacementSolver with the CurrentSenseShuntSeparatedFromInputs advisory to identify and report local low-value shunts connected across current-sense amplifier inputs when they are improperly placed, ensuring correct sensing connections. |
| [#170](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/170) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | imrishabh18 | Detects overlapping trace-generated schematic text labels and includes them in collision detection while excluding componentsymbol-owned text. |
| [#169](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/169) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | imrishabh18 | Detects overlapping trace-generated schematic text labels by including schematic_text records with source_trace_id in text-clearance detection, reporting SchematicTextCollision issues for overlaps. |
| [#165](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/165) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Fixes the issue where vertical supply-to-signal resistors were skipped by the TwoPinComponentHasInvertedRails check due to the lack of a ground connection, by extending the check to include typed resistors with an explicitly positive supply. |
| [#150](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/150) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Detects and corrects misplaced pull-up and mixed switchresistor orientations in the RUN layout, ensuring proper placement of components based on their orientation and type. |
| [#180](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/180) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | MustafaMulla29 | Fixes false component overlaps caused by schematic annotation boxes by excluding unowned annotation boxes from component placement analysis and converting standalone box coordinates to placement centers. |
| [#138](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-schematic-placement-analysis/pull/138) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | MustafaMulla29 | Adds MosfetGateNetworkPlacementSolver to report MosfetGateNetworkNotGrouped when gate resistors are separated from their MOSFET, requiring explicit roles and same schematic scope. |

References Renesas 8V41NS0412 Evaluation Board User Guide, Figure 5 on page 10(https:www.renesas.comendocumentmah8v41ns0412-evaluation-board-user-guide) shows a four-pin crystal with pins 1 and 3 used for the oscillator signals, pins 2 and 4 grounded, and a load capacitor from each signal to ground. ### [tscircuit/cli](https://github.com/tscircuit/cli)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |
|------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| [#5094](https://github.com/tscircuit/cli/pull/5094) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Prevents attribute merging between pins when compact pin aliases collide by maintaining original generated TSX and providing a CLI explanation for exact pin conflicts. |
| [#5066](https://github.com/tscircuit/cli/pull/5066) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | tsci import leaves out electrical attributes stored in the datasheet API, including the F1C100S AVCC pins 2.8 V requirement and the AP2127K-2.8TRG1 regulators 2.8 V output. Look up the imported components exact manufacturer part number through the configured registrys datasheet endpoint, validate the returned attributes with the existing props schema, and put them on Circuit JSON source ports. Generate the enriched component with released circuit-json-to-tscircuit0.0.50, which now emits pinAttributes directly. This uses the live API and does not depend on parts-engine PR 60. There is no TSX rewrite for pin attributes. Absent or empty metadata keeps the existing EasyEDA conversion path. Invalid metadata, a mismatched part, failed requests, or the 5-second lookup timeout warn and continue with the existing import. The deadline covers response headers and body; a timeout warns that the datasheet API did not respond within 5 seconds and pinAttributes may not be populated. Other lookup failures also warn that attributes may not be populated. Physical pin entries override signal-label entries; existing Circuit JSON fields are preserved. Exactcompact footprints, pin labels, suppliermanufacturer metadata, CAD references, and caller props overrides remain supported. Use tsci import C460327 --exclude-pin-attributes to skip the datasheet lookup and use the existing EasyEDA import path. Importer-inferred attributes (such as ground pins) remain. The flag is covered for both search-result imports and direct part-number fallback. Only the existing converter devDependency is upgraded; no packages are added. Validation: 20 new tests pass, including all 89 F1C100S pins and five regulator pins in both exact and compact footprint modes, canonical props validation, CAD references, caller overrides, namedphysical pin precedence, falsezero values, capabilities, and missingfailing datasheets. Typecheck, build, and formatting pass. Live CLI imports of C1511928 and C460327 match every attribute in the production datasheet API. Both generated components render with the expected 89five schematic ports; AVCC requires 2.8 V and regulator pin 5 provides 2.8 V. Rendering these isolated, unwired chips reports only the expected must-be-connected errors. The broader local import suite still hits the previously reproduced baseline dependency error: tscircuitprops does not export assemblySubassemblyProps for the footprint rendering test. This failure was also reproduced on unchanged main; dependency versions unrelated to the converter are unchanged. Uses https:github.comtscircuitcircuit-json-to-tscircuitpull121 and replaces the closed CLI-specific TSX rewrite in https:github.comtscircuitclipull5063. |
| [#5129](https://github.com/tscircuit/cli/pull/5129) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | tsci import now passes validated datasheet pinAttributes through EasyEDAs native Circuit JSON and TSX converters. This preserves attributes keyed by physical pin numbers or BGA balls, including explicit empty rows, while retaining the supplier component type, custom schematic symbol, footprint and CAD settings. Use the published easyeda0.0.369 API from easyeda-converter590(https:github.comtscircuiteasyeda-converterpull590) and remove the CLI-owned attribute mapper and generic TSX conversion branch. The existing datasheet lookup, schema validation, five-second deadline, failure warning and --exclude-pin-attributes flag remain. No new dependencies are added. Validation: Five new import regressions cover all 29 DRV8818PWPR physical rows in exact and compact footprints, numericprefixed overrides with false and zero values, explicit empty rows, untouched-pin inference, a custom supplier symbol and pushbutton classification. Existing F1C100S, i.MX6 BGA, exclusion and stalled headersbody tests remain. Import test group: 37 passed, 1 existing skipped, 0 failed. Frozen-lockfile install, production build, TypeScript, repository formatting and dependency-policy checks pass. Actual CLI imports using the published EasyEDA release and live datasheet API pass for all 47 available stepper supplier geometries plus F1C100S: 1,256 physical contacts and 3,095 populated fields preserved, with zero warnings or mismatches. All four documented empty rows remain empty. All 48 outputs match their --exclude-pin-attributes counterparts after removing only attributes, preserving footprint, labels, component type and CAD settings. F1C100S retains all 89 physical contacts and 366 stored fields. |

| [#868](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json/pull/868) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Add optional required_voltage_tolerance to SourcePinAttributes to retain voltage requirements tolerance when Circuit JSON is saved, loaded, or checked outside core. |
| [#866](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json/pull/866) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Add cad_cable to represent a physical assembly cable independently of its electrical pin mapping, including cable name, resolved source-component endpoints, cableprinter definition string, and a sampled world-space centerline. |
| [#864](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json/pull/864) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Add printedpart, subassembly, and motor as source_component ftypes so assembly parts can retain their source identity and CAD references without being labeled simple_chip. |
| [#858](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json/pull/858) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Normalizes requires_voltage and provides_voltage attributes to numeric volts, allowing for direct comparison of electrical values without string interpretation. |
| [#854](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json/pull/854) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Add pcb_soldermask_opening for defining solder-mask openings in PCB designs, allowing for flexible coverlay windows that span multiple pads without adding copper or solder paste. |
| [#850](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json/pull/850) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Adds missing attributes for direction-only pins in SourcePinAttributes to preserve F1C100S pin attributes during datasheet enrichment. |
| [#847](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json/pull/847) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Add a CAD collision error schema to record mechanical collisions in Circuit JSON, including affected references and area measurements. |

### [tscircuit/props](https://github.com/tscircuit/props)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |

| [#901](https://github.com/tscircuit/props/pull/901) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Put routing intent directly on bus and differentialpair. A bus names its associated pair with lengthMatchTo; its maxLengthSkew applies to the combined signals while the pair can retain its own tighter skew. Shared target lengths refer to existing signalsgroups, without another grouping element. tsx bus nameDATA0 connections...dataSignals, maskSignal lengthMatchTo.DQS0 maxLengthSkew25mil maxLength reference: longest_manhattan  pcbTraceSpacing3w pcbSpacingToOtherSignals4w targetImpedance5025ohm   Impedance input is scalarXML-compatible: numeric ohms, a unit string, or an absolute tolerance string. targetImpedance5025ohm normalizes to nominal 50 ohms and flat MinMax bounds 2575 ohms. Alternatively, declare targetImpedanceMin25ohm targetImpedanceMax75ohm without a nominal target. Differential pairs support the equivalent targetDifferentialImpedanceMinMax props. Existing scalar targets keep their scalar output, object impedance ranges are rejected, and conflicting boundstolerance notations are rejected. pcbEscapeSpacing is removed for now. Adds direct minLength, maxLength, targetLength, lengthTolerance, pcbTraceSpacing and pcbSpacingToOtherSignals props. Relative length expressions and width-relative spacing retain their geometry intent; no electrical defaults, protocol profile, new JSX element, expected-member-count field, check-only grouping API or generic constraint wrapper is introduced. Existing connectionspair polarity supply membership; existing pinAttributes remain available for pin-level electrical intent. The existing relative length object API remains unchanged in this update. See docspcb-bus-routing.md for datastrobe and commandclock authoring examples, accepted inputs, normalized output, conflicts and migration. Validation: all 610 local tests, type check and package build pass. All four documentation generators run. CI is green: tests, types, formatting and package preview build pass. CorechecksCircuit JSON PRs 4346374862 still need adaptation to this author-facing API; this props PR does not claim runtime enforcement or hardware compliance. |

### [tscircuit/easyeda-converter](https://github.com/tscircuit/easyeda-converter)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |
|------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| [#590](https://github.com/tscircuit/easyeda-converter/pull/590) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Callers currently cannot supply reviewed datasheet pin attributes to EasyEDA conversion. This forces tsci import to merge attributes itself and use a separate TSX conversion path, where numeric datasheet keys can be lost. Add optional caller-supplied pinAttributes to Circuit JSON conversion and both rawparsed TSX entry points, using the existing props schema. Resolve attributes against actual footprint pad identifiers, including BGA balls, and write them onto source_port records before using the normal TSX generator. Physical rows take precedence over signal aliases; explicit empty rows, false values, zero quantities and serial capabilities survive. Existing symbol selection, component classification, footprint geometry and CAD handling are preserved. The caller retains responsibility for fetching and validating datasheet metadata. There are no new runtime dependencies or datasheet API requests. Existing development versions of props, tscircuit and circuit-json-util are updated to support the current schemas; one pushbutton schematic snapshot is refreshed for the current native symbol position. Validation: 12 focused tests  233 assertions, including all 29 DRV8818 pads, BGA identifiers, repeated pads, alias conflicts, explicit uncertainty and a semantic TypeScript check for capability arrays. Normal EasyEDA conversion preserves all 2,729 attribute fields across 47 real stepper-driver imports  1,167 physical contacts. Strict props and Circuit JSON validation pass; geometry, labels, symbols, classification and CAD metadata match the conversion without supplied attributes. TypeScript, Biome and NodebrowserCLI builds with declaration files pass. No-option generated TSX is byte-identical to the original generator for all ten component types. The full suite completed with 317 passing tests and eight snapshot failures. The pushbutton snapshot was reviewed, refreshed and its test passes. The remaining seven CAD snapshot failures reproduce with the original conversion code on the same development runtime; all 19 compared TSXCircuit JSON outputs are byte-identical. A renderer OBJ request returning HTTP 403 HTML was observed for C2886621, resulting in an empty CAD mesh. Those seven reference images are intentionally retained. This provides the upstream API needed to replace the CLI-owned merge and alternate conversion path discussed in tscircuitcli5129. |
| [#591](https://github.com/tscircuit/easyeda-converter/pull/591) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | AnasSarkiz | Importing C105188 (TLV3201AIDBVR), C94934 (TPD2EUSB30ADRTR), C20917 (AO3400A), or C15127 (AO3401A) produces custom schematic artwork without the component reference label. This PR captures that behavior before the fix with raw EasyEDA fixtures and schematic SVG snapshots of two differently named instances of each part. C94934 also verifies that its existing GND annotation is preserved. The tests intentionally assert the current missing-label behavior; the stacked fix will change those assertions to require each instances reference text. Validation: all four new reproductions pass; formatting, TypeScript checks, and build pass. The full local suite had 320 passes, seven existing 3D snapshot failures, and two 5-second timeouts. Rerunning the affected existing tests with CIs 60-second timeout exposes eight repeatable pre-existing 3D snapshot mismatches; the new reproductions still pass. No existing 3D snapshots or converter implementation are changed. |
| [#587](https://github.com/tscircuit/easyeda-converter/pull/587) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | AnasSarkiz | Fixes the issue where current-sense resistors are imported without electrical resistance due to incorrect symbol prefix classification. |
| [#578](https://github.com/tscircuit/easyeda-converter/pull/578) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | GokulPandi-M | Fixes the incorrect classification of the VBUS power pin in the USB hub board, eliminating misleading missing-power warnings for components connected to VBUS. |

| [#402](https://github.com/tscircuit/jscad-electronics/pull/402) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Add parameterized external spur gears and worm screws to the React and vanilla renderers, including Footprinter3d model-string routing, standalone JSCAD solids, and indexed mesh factories. Mechanical gear strings also work through the with-pads API without generating PCB copper. Examples: ts spurgear24_m1mm_w5mm_bore5mm_hubdiameter10mm_hublength3mm wormgear_m1mm_d10mm_l20mm_starts2_left_bore3mm  Spur gears use sampled involute flanks, circular tooth tipsroot valleys, optional through bores and integrated hubs. Worm screws sweep a trapezoidal axial rack profile, supporting one to eight starts and either hand. Both support phase and resolution controls, adapt root faceting to preserve thin borehub walls, and reject mesh allocations above one million vertices. The guide documents pitch conventions and approximation limits: spur root filletsundercut and conjugate worm wheels are outside this initial implementation. Depends on https:github.comtscircuitmodelprinterpull11. The manifest and lockfile pin its published gear-enabled package preview; replace that preview with a released ModelPrinter version after the spec lands. Validation: bun test --timeout 10000 passes all 323 tests, and build, TypeScript and formatting pass. Geometry regressions cover manifold edges, outward winding, positive triangle areasvolume, independent involute and helical profile measurements, handedness, startslead, bores, near-root walls, allocation limits, large phases, and Reactvanilla parity. Eight Cosmos variants and three four-view PNG snapshots cover the new models. |
| [#403](https://github.com/tscircuit/jscad-electronics/pull/403) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Adds HelicalGear, indexed meshJSCAD factories, and helicalgear routing through React, Footprinter3d, and vanilla without PCB pads. |

Model strings, schemas, dimensions, defaults, and validation come from modelprinter PR 7; this repository owns the models and visual tests. The motors have accurately positioned mounting bores, blind-hole floors or through-flange holes, pilots, and configurable roundD shafts. Tests probe the rendered solids at the mounting centers and shaft flats, including custom dimensions and rotated flats. Motor defaults and coordinate conventions are documented in modelprinter. HexSocketBolt and SheetMetal components and JSCAD solid factories wrap the migrated indexed surfaces without changing their geometry. Existing mesh factories and types are exported from jscad-electronics. Footprinter3d and vanilla helpers route these mechanical strings and return no PCB pads. Geometry topology checks and four-view poppygl snapshots move here; the migrated PNGs were reviewed with this repositorys renderer versions. Node ESM tests cover the built vanilla models. The dependency is pinned to the modelprinter spec PR commit so CI uses the real contract without duplicate defaults or parsers. Release order: publish the updated modelprinter package first, then replace the Git pin with that published version before releasing jscad-electronics. Sets Node 24 for Vercel; its previous Node 20 project default was discontinued. Validation: all 294 tests pass; typecheck, format check, library build, and Cosmos site build pass. A fresh consumer install verifies Git preparation and Node loading. GitHub Actions and Vercel are green on the final PR commit (cbcbf48). |

Components forward material settings to the reconciler without withMaterialwithMaterialProp wrappers. There are no README changes. Materials survive rendering, plan execution, transforms, extrusions, and subtraction, and both fiber Three.js rendering paths use the material settings. ### [tscircuit/checks](https://github.com/tscircuit/checks)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |
|------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| [#375](https://github.com/tscircuit/checks/pull/375) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Add routed DDR constraint snapshots that compare passing and mutated copper for DDR byte-lane routing, highlighting violations and providing detailed measurements. |
| [#374](https://github.com/tscircuit/checks/pull/374) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Check length, spacing and impedance intent directly from source_bus, matching the natural bus and differential-pair props published in props 901. Length matching includes resolved external traces without changing electrical membership. Lengths come from complete pad-to-pad copper geometry, including planar fanouts; cached lengths and guessed via barrel depths are excluded. Spacing checks include partial copper and unrelated signals outside declared buses, use the larger local width for width multipliers, and exclude declared differential partners and same-net fragments. No reduced escape-spacing allowance is inferred. Violations use specific pcb_bus_routing_constraint_error elements; missing geometry and physical impedance analysis use separate pcb_bus_routing_constraint_warning elements, so an unverified rule cannot look like a pass. Diagnostics are discriminated by routing_rule, with required rule-specific measurement names. Lengths and centerline spacing are mm, impedance is ohms; no units property is emitted. Spacing errors include the measured separation and offending PCB trace. Validation: 14 focused geometryconstraint tests pass. Circuit JSON 862 is merged and this PR now uses published circuit-json 0.0.514. CI validates the complete suite and package build against that release. Core integration follows in 4346; these checks do not certify an electrical interface or replace physical stackup and signal-integrity analysis. |
| [#372](https://github.com/tscircuit/checks/pull/372) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | User-facing diagnostics currently expose Circuit JSON IDs directly or through readable-name helpers that fall back to IDs. Use readable elementcomponentpinnet names throughout the checks, with honest labels such as unnamed via and unnamed bend when names are unavailable. Diagnostic IDs and object-reference fields remain unchanged. |

| [#483](https://github.com/tscircuit/schematic-symbols/pull/483) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Adds _sm and _xs variants for diodes, LEDs, avalanche diodes, and Zener diodes in right, left, up, and down orientations: 32 symbols total. These match the existing compact passive naming and 0.5 mm  0.35 mm pin spans. Shorter leads retain readable diode bodies, LED emission arrows, and the distinct avalancheZener cathode bars. LED annotations have extra clearance for the arrows. All new variants use 1posanode and 2negcathode aliases; existing symbols are unchanged. Includes source SVGs, generated geometry and exports, 32 SVG snapshots, and usage documentation. Regression coverage checks pin spans, polarity, connected leads, closed triangles, and diode body proportions. Validation: 26 tests pass (2,237 assertions); build, TypeScript, formatting, snapshot validation, and diff checks pass. Rendered variants were visually inspected. |

### [tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |
|------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| [#121](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit/pull/121) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Circuit JSON can contain a pins electrical requirements, but converting it to a chip currently drops them. This loses information such as the F1C100S AVCC pin requiring 2.8 V and the AP2127K-2.8TRG1 output providing 2.8 V. Generate the chips pinAttributes directly from source_port records. Map electrical fields to the tscircuitprops names and convert supportedconfigured capability flags into capabilitiesactiveCapabilities. Preserve explicit false and zero values, use physical pin numbers when available and names otherwise, omit empty attributes, and keep caller overrides working. Avoid combining multiple components attributes into one chip. No dependencies or lockfile changes. Validation: Five new regression tests cover all 89 F1C100S pins and five regulator pins, directionoutput modes, pull resistors, capabilities, numericstring voltages and capacitances, missing attributes, named pins, component isolation, and caller overrides. Live parts-engine  enriched Circuit JSON  converter  TSX verification preserves every attribute from both production datasheets (capability arrays compared as sets). Generated attributes validate against the current tscircuitprops schema, including the correct 2.8 V requirement and output. Full suite: 37 tests pass, zero failures (the existing test.failing for dropped board children accounts for the expected failed snapshot). Typecheck, build, and formatting pass. Pairs with https:github.comtscircuitparts-enginepull60 and the merged Circuit JSON schema extension https:github.comtscircuitcircuit-jsonpull850. This moves TSX generation into the converter; downstream CLI integration should consume enriched Circuit JSON instead of rewriting generated source as in https:github.comtscircuitclipull5063. |
| [#132](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit/pull/132) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | ShiboSoftwareDev | Preserves the fabrication dimension offset distance and direction through the native primitive, ensuring no synthetic extension paths are emitted and preventing duplicate renderer lines. |
| [#131](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit/pull/131) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | ShiboSoftwareDev | Summary preserve complete board schematics instead of collapsing them into one generic chip convert components, net labels, primitives, and traces through native tscircuit schematic elements rather than SVG retain source coordinates and styling, with focused regressions and four real TI EVM visual comparisons supersedes 92  Testing bun test: 56 passed bun run build bun run format:check |
| [#125](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit/pull/125) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | ShiboSoftwareDev | Summary convert BRep silkscreen artwork into native silkscreengraphic elements retain filled outlines, interior holes, placement, and board side refresh the three affected real TI EVM round-trip comparisons  Test plan bun test bunx tsc --noEmit bun run format:check |
| [#124](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit/pull/124) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | ShiboSoftwareDev | Summary emit native copperpour and net elements for authored copper regions preserve rectangular, polygon, and BRep outer boundaries plus layer and solder-mask coverage refresh all four real TI EVM round-trip comparisons  Test plan bun test bunx tsc --noEmit bun run format:check |
| [#123](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit/pull/123) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | ShiboSoftwareDev | Summary emit native pcbtrace elements for authored PCB routes preserve every wire and via route point without rerouting refresh all four real TI EVM round-trip comparisons  Test plan bun test bunx tsc --noEmit bun run format:check |
| [#126](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-tscircuit/pull/126) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | KrishnaX12 | Motivation PCB vias should survive Circuit JSON  tscircuit round trips. The TI EVM repros in 89 showed vias in the source board but omitted them from the generated boardfor example, all 109 LM5155 vias disappeared.  Before The footprint converter handled pads and plated holes but had no handler for pcb_via. Those records never became JSX, so the generated board lost the via arrays visible around components. !Before: LM5155 source and generated board with missing vias(https:raw.githubusercontent.comtscircuitcircuit-json-to-tscircuit47cdc0185b792a531c50bf7ea0e56a1532c2629fteststi-evms__snapshots__ti-lm5155evm-fly-roundtrip-pcb-comparison.snap.svg)  After Register convertVias alongside the existing footprint converters. Each source via becomes a via with its position, hole and copper diameters, layer endpoints, and tenting. Support the legacy is_tented field emitted by altium-to-circuit-json at the fixture generation commit(https:github.comtscircuitaltium-to-circuit-jsonblob9a578b5762e1dcca06e6d3f84f1d2950d0acc301libpcbroutingconvertPcbVia.ts), with explicit per-side tenting taking precedence. Reject vias without resolvable layer endpoints with an error naming the via. The four TI EVM round trips now preserve all source vias: LM5155 (109), LM251772 (261), DRV8307 (360), and LMG342 (411). PCB and inline TSX snapshots are refreshed; schematic snapshots are unchanged. !After: LM5155 source and generated board with restored vias(https:raw.githubusercontent.comKrishnaX12circuit-json-to-tscircuitff357d2a02004c418bc45f767155ac19bcf64bc9teststi-evms__snapshots__ti-lm5155evm-fly-roundtrip-pcb-comparison.snap.svg)  Validation Regression tests verify through, blind, and buried via geometry, layers, tenting, and missing-layer rejection. All four TI EVM tests compare rendered via counts against the source counts; all four PCB comparisons were visually checked. Additional runtime checks verify reversed layer endpoints and all supported tenting modes, including modern flags overriding legacy tenting. GitHub CI passes Bun tests, Type Check, and Format Check on ff357d2. The local snapshot-summary anomaly also occurs on the unchanged base, which exits successfully; all affected snapshots pass. Vercel preview requires maintainer authorization. |

Library resistors and capacitors in 89 render with 0.02 mm outlines, but the converter copied the symbol librarys 0.012 mm path width. That made 230 symbols across the four TI fixtures 40 thinner after conversion (43 DRV8307, 87 LM251772, 54 LM5155, 46 LMG342X). Use the same documented component-outline width for library paths and circles. This extends the circle styling from 151: circuit-to-svg deliberately uses this fixed width regardless of the library primitive width or symbol scale. There are no board names, component IDs, or geometry heuristics in the implementation. Explicit standalone schematic path widths remain unchanged. The existing test20 expected-failure test still prints a failed-snapshot summary while the test run exits successfully. All four full TI board round-trip tests pass; regenerated schematic and generated-TSX snapshots are included. PCB snapshots are unchanged. The local full-board runs used temporary 600-second timeouts, restored before commit. TypeScript, build, formatting, and git diff whitespace checks pass.  Snapshot diff The SVG changes are limited to stroke widths and insignificant whitespace. Each image contains the original schematic on the left and the round-trip result on the right. | [#921](https://github.com/tscircuit/docs/pull/921) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Changes the default behavior of 3D previews in documentation to use realistic lighting, enhancing the visual quality of hosted 3D previews. |
| [#919](https://github.com/tscircuit/docs/pull/919) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Add an Advanced Assembly guide demonstrating the new motor face-mounting API with a NEMA17 attached to a printed L bracket, then a controller mounted on the same base. Both CircuitPreview examples show the assembly TSX first and include complete bracketcontroller source in file tabs. Checked-in 3D renders and Circuit JSON come from the same source maps, with a regeneration script. Explain named reference faces, orientation, clearances, matching mounting holes, and PCB anchoring; link the guide from the motor and printed-part references and document the motor mounting props. Validation: Docs typecheck and production build pass. Both examples pass tsci build and TypeScript checking against published core 0.0.2085 and props 0.0.687. Published CLI CADboardhole geometry matches the checked-in previews. Verified file tabs, 3D previews, and controller PCB view in the browser. |

Both the 47-signal example and the separate 89-pad VCCGND dogbone study now render through svg.tscircuit.com from fsMap. Remove all four static PCB assets, the static layer gallery, and the pcbPreviewUrl override. The examples use the public bus_lanes preset, buses, and differential pairs. Local dogbones are automatic only for unrouted pad endpoints; existing fanout exits are preserved. No custom algorithm, saved route geometry, or new props API is used. The AM3352 example requires published core 0.0.2030 or later, now deployed by the SVG service. Validation: production docs build, typecheck, and all docs CI checks pass. The exact live SVG URL shape used by CircuitPreview returned a routed image from production (HTTP 200, imagesvgxml, cache MISS), which was visually inspected. Cold rendering took 91.6 seconds: the under-30-second performance target is still unresolved. The power examples live SVG rendered in 6.8 seconds; its separate production Circuit JSON check contained 89 tracesvias and zero DRC errors. The full signal fixture passes all 169 core routingDRCquality assertions locally. Preview pages: DDR guide  AM3352 example(https:docs-git-docs-bus-lanes-am3352-guide-tscircuit.vercel.appguidesrouting-ddram3352-bus-lanes) Autorouting phase reference(https:docs-git-docs-bus-lanes-am3352-guide-tscircuit.vercel.appelementsautoroutingphaseroute-bus-lanes-without-layer-changes) Board reference(https:docs-git-docs-bus-lanes-am3352-guide-tscircuit.vercel.appelementsboard) |
| [#907](https://github.com/tscircuit/docs/pull/907) | 🐌 Tiny | seveibar | Presents the AM3352-to-DDR3 section as a practical bus_lanes example, removing unnecessary commentary and clarifying component descriptions. |
| [#909](https://github.com/tscircuit/docs/pull/909) | 🐌 Tiny | seveibar | Reduces the size of the AM3352 routing example board from 70  70 mm to 22  44 mm, centering the outline around the chips and their routes, and moving the annotation inside the tighter outline. |
| [#902](https://github.com/tscircuit/docs/pull/902) | 🐌 Tiny | seveibar | Document local dogbone fanout with 36-pin BGA footprinter examples in the fanout element reference, clarifying limitations and updating links. |
| [#905](https://github.com/tscircuit/docs/pull/905) | 🐌 Tiny | seveibar | Removes the Start from a form factor section and its Arduino shield example from the AI circuit-generation guide, along with the unused CircuitPreview import. |
| [#903](https://github.com/tscircuit/docs/pull/903) | 🐌 Tiny | seveibar | Restricts the AI callout to only appear in specified introductory and getting-started documentation pages, requiring explicit front matter configuration. |
| [#897](https://github.com/tscircuit/docs/pull/897) | 🐌 Tiny | seveibar | Document direct modelUrl imports on all four assembly elements, including device models at the world origin and displays placed relative to connectors. Lead the subassembly reference with grouping CAD children, and show imported screws aligned with real board holes. All eight code examples across the four element references and mounting guide use CircuitPreview, defaulting to 3D. Partial snippets are expanded into complete circuits. Original demo GLB models replace placeholder URLs, with asset URLs pinned to a committed revision so previews work before deployment. The hosted SVG evaluator is pinned to core from before the assembly API and returns an undefined-element error for these examples. An optional circuitJson prop lets CircuitPreview render data compiled from the displayed source while preserving the original code and editor link. Checked-in preview data includes a regeneration script and instructions; existing previews keep their default behavior. The props and core dependencies are merged: https:github.comtscircuitpropspull872 and https:github.comtscircuitcorepull4221. Validation: bun run typecheck and bun run build pass. Regenerated all eight examples with the updated core and verified their emitted CAD models. Requested the hosted 3D renders and visually inspected the screw placement, housing, cover, bracket, and display. No fenced TSX snippets remain in these five docs; every code example uses CircuitPreview. |

### [tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf)

| PR # | Impact | Rating | Contributor | Description |
|------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| [#237](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/237) | 🐳 Major | ⭐⭐⭐ | seveibar | Multi-board assemblies previously exported only the first PCB, leaving the other boards connector models floating in space. Export every board at its own world position with its own thickness, holes, cutouts, and texture data. Browser exports use the full conversion path for multiple boards. Board textures are assigned before material caching, so another board cannot reuse and overwrite the first boards material. Previously both boards showed the second boards footprint image even though PCB and CAD coordinates agreed. Adds a regression fixture from the USB-C hostcontroller docs example and checks both board meshes, textures, cable geometry, GLB export, and browser exports without cables. The generated docs render was inspected with both boards and the cable visible. Docs example: tscircuitdocs920. |
| [#236](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/236) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Render cad_cable records using jscad-electronics physical connector and cable meshes, preserving per-mesh colors and supporting cable-only scenes as well as assemblies containing boards. |
| [#234](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/234) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Updates jscad-electronics to 0.0.182 to enable modelprinter NEMA strings to produce motor geometry in circuit GLTFGLB exports and adds assembly regression tests for NEMA8, NEMA17, and NEMA23 frames. |
| [#233](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/233) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Boards with material: flex and no explicit solder-mask color currently render green. Resolve their default surface and edge colors to the polyimide amber used by 3d-viewer, including the no-bend browser path. Explicit mask, background, side and silkscreen colors retain priority; empty and not_specified mask values use the material default. |
| [#232](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/232) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Updates the PCB bending functionality to support independent and nested nonparallel bends, enhancing the flexibility of PCB design. |
| [#230](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/230) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Fixes GLTFGLB export failures for unsupported PCB bends and rigid objects crossing a bend zone by retaining existing flat geometry for declared limitations while allowing unexpected errors to propagate. |
| [#229](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/229) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Fixes the issue where the U-shaped board cannot export its finite left-tail fold due to the exporter creating a fold without the board outline, by adopting tscircuitflex-utils 0.0.6 and passing the correct board outline after translation. |
| [#231](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/231) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | seveibar | Adds opt-in metadata to display Circuit JSON errors in 3D exports using PoppyGLs debug labels, preserving existing geometry and materials. |
| [#224](https://github.com/tscircuit/circuit-json-to-gltf/pull/224) | 🐙 Minor | ⭐⭐ | addibble | Fixes CAD model export rotation directions for X and Y axes, ensuring correct upright positioning of components in the receptacle. |

This reduces that work while preserving candidate order and contact choices: Reuse generation-stamped bitsets to enumerate spatial candidates in ascending original-index order. Use a stable first-minimum contact scan, computing each distance once and preserving ties. Reject distant obstacles before connectivitylayer predicates. Allocate via-group visited sets lazily and remove redundant deduplication of disjoint ranges. This main-targeting branch starts at 6f6e5dbfc1eb5616f2b81b4ace76884c85d572b5. It preserves mains diameter parameter and observed unique layer order in collectViaNodes, including a 3, 1, 2 stack. It excludes unrelated fixes from the older autorouter-pinned branch. The controlled full-repair replay compares exact historical ccad3906aee0ef2 worktrees with the same transitive dependencies and fresh native ConnectivityMap 0.0.19 for both. Three sequential alternating timed runs per variant gave 26.325118.9977 s (27.8 lower median). All eight runs, including warmups, had identical routes, 16 iterations, repair statistics, and independent DRC output. Sourcefixture checks, all samples, provenance, and reproduction steps are committed under benchmarksrepair-isolation. The official repair-only consumer autorouter 2827(https:github.comtscircuittscircuit-autorouterpull2827) measured 4.318 lower P50 (95.88591.744 s) and 3.146 lower total elapsed time, with all 16 cases completing and passing relaxed DRC, zero issues and timeouts. Both this official result and the controlled full-repair replay concern the original ccadaee pins, not the current-main port. The benchmarked pinned branch stays in 154. Run bun run benchmark:spatial-index --out tmprepair-spatial-benchmark.json to compare original Setsort grid, production bitset grid, Flatbush and RBush over nine deterministic distributions, a labeled static Pipeline9 fixture, and four actual captured repair windows. Production-compatible mode indexes integer cell rectangles and checks every complete sorted output against a brute-force oracle. Physical-AABB mode is separately labeled because moving geometry prevents it from being an equivalent production replacement. Tree packages are development-only; constructionquery costs, nine trials, ranges, checksums, and installed versions are recorded. On this main ports two captured segment windows, bitset query medians fell 63.962.2. Summed separate buildquery medians across all four windows fell 25.354910.2059 ms (59.7). Flatbush won small via windows and long-rectangle generated cases; bitsets lost on sparse scattered-ID queries at 16,384 items. These are index-workload measurements, not full dataset18 proof. All samples and interpretation are under benchmarksspatial-index. A benchmark-only same-aee full-repair backend comparison gave 17.5192 s bitsets vs 21.4458 s Flatbush (22.4 more tree-adapter time) with identical outputsstatisticsindependent DRC across all six runs. This limited experiment used two alternating measured runs per variant plus warmups and includes bounds-snapshot conversioncounters. RBush is measured in the index harness; no full-stage RBush timing was run. Earlier single-pass diagnostics observed 39.71630.17624.435 s: the combined bitsetobstaclevia-allocation group saved 9.540s, then stable first-minimum selection saved 5.741s. Those runs used the previous sharedindexed connectivity setup, had possible local contention, and are not the newer native-connectivity isolation. The groupedmarginal observations cannot establish independent causal shares for each change. Local validation: 96 tests  3,955 assertions passed, full TypeScript checking including all three benchmark runners and USB fixture, and full repository format check. Independent fairness reviews verified ordered oracle parity, identical build boundaries, and clearly labeled construction estimates. The already-mocked portfolio incumbent regression now replays its genuine native CC1 constructor input, with capture provenance, while retaining every assertion and both live USB integration tests. No test timeout was changed. The complete suite also passes under CIs Bun 1.3.1. |

This change reuses that immutable geometry and exposes deterministic work limits so Pipeline9 can bound unsuccessful searches. Dynamic buckets, route order and global via ownership are rebuilt for each evaluation; the dependency flags remain disabled by default. Reuse up to 128 exact duplicate proposal scores until an accepted route changes. Validate candidate copper and mandatory via-pad clearance before indexed scoring. Add optional maxCandidateAttempts and maxPathSearchNodes. Attempts include yielded proposals rejected by permissions; nodes count actual heap pops across searches and accepted states. Budget completion retains validated progress and reports remaining DRC. Invalid geometry and solver failures still surface. Repair operates on the supplied bounded region. Endpoint and boundary-collar protection, fixed copper, via permissions, new via-pad rejection, candidate order and DRC acceptance rules are preserved. Default search budgets remain unchanged in this package; Pipeline9 selects its policy. Validation at 91644859f79c001db96f1e41fd38dfd713731a15: 62 tests  10,632 assertions, typecheck, and formatting passed on their first attempts. The installed 121-input source graph differs from the preceding revision only in Repair04Solver.ts and AutoroutingDrcEngine.ts; all 301 installed package manifests are unchanged. The dependency passed 103 tests  1,945 assertions. Its final default and opt-in paths matched ordered errors and statistics across 800 saved-input comparisons, including reordered traces and mutation of previously returned results. Earlier cache stages also matched 21,072 synthetic solver step states and repeated guard cases.
